Motivation: 现实世界中的多机器人协调任务需要处理大量传感器数据，而全面传输这些数据会压垮网络。需要一种系统级方法来优化资源使用，同时确保任务成功。

Method: 提出R2X框架，将高层自然语言意图作为资源优化的过滤器，通过MLLM理解目标，选择性激活相关感知模态，动态分配带宽，确定计算放置位置，联合优化感知、通信和计算。

Result: 展示了四个端到端演示：数字孪生仓库导航、移动驱动的主动MCS控制、带语义感知开关的FollowMe机器人、边缘辅助MLLM接地实现的真实硬件开放词汇垃圾分类。系统级指标显示R2X编排优于纯设备端基线。

Conclusion: R2X作为意图到资源的编排框架，通过集成设计实现了MLLM指导下的多机器人协调，在资源约束下最大化任务级成功率，展示了系统级优化的必要性。

Abstract: Imagine advanced humanoid robots, powered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), coordinating missions across industries like warehouse logistics, manufacturing, and safety rescue. While individual robots show local autonomy, realistic tasks demand coordination among multiple agents sharing vast streams of sensor data. Communication is indispensable, yet transmitting comprehensive data can overwhelm networks, especially when a system-level orchestrator or cloud-based MLLM fuses multimodal inputs for route planning or anomaly detection.
  These tasks are often initiated by high-level natural language instructions. This intent serves as a filter for resource optimization: by understanding the goal via MLLMs, the system can selectively activate relevant sensing modalities, dynamically allocate bandwidth, and determine computation placement. Thus, R2X is fundamentally an intent-to-resource orchestration problem where sensing, communication, and computation are jointly optimized to maximize task-level success under resource constraints.
  This survey examines how integrated design paves the way for multi-robot coordination under MLLM guidance. We review state-of-the-art sensing modalities, communication strategies, and computing approaches, highlighting how reasoning is split between on-device models and powerful edge/cloud servers. We present four end-to-end demonstrations (sense -> communicate -> compute -> act): (i) digital-twin warehouse navigation with predictive link context, (ii) mobility-driven proactive MCS control, (iii) a FollowMe robot with a semantic-sensing switch, and (iv) real-hardware open-vocabulary trash sorting via edge-assisted MLLM grounding. We emphasize system-level metrics -- payload, latency, and success -- to show why R2X orchestration outperforms purely on-device baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Long-Horizon Geometry-Aware Navigation among Polytopes via MILP-MPC and Minkowski-Based CBFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00162)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Salman Ghori,Ania Adil,Eric Feron,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划控制框架，结合MILP-MPC长时程轨迹规划和基于精确符号距离的CBF安全滤波器，实现多面体机器人在多面体障碍环境中的几何感知安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂非凸环境中，同时考虑机器人动力学、控制限制和精确几何形状的自主导航仍然具有挑战性。现有方法难以在计算效率和几何安全保证之间取得平衡。

Method: 分层框架：高层使用MILP-MPC生成点质量机器人的名义轨迹；低层使用基于Minkowski差空间精确符号距离的CBF作为安全滤波器，并扩展到高阶CBF，强制执行机器人几何约束。

Result: 在U形和迷宫环境中，针对单积分器和双积分器动力学进行了验证。该框架减轻了纯反应式CBF导航的拓扑诱导局部最小值问题，实现了安全、实时、几何感知的导航。

Conclusion: 提出的分层规划控制框架成功地将长时程轨迹规划与几何感知安全保证相结合，为多面体机器人在复杂障碍环境中的导航提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex, non-convex environments remains challenging when robot dynamics, control limits, and exact robot geometry must all be taken into account. In this paper, we propose a hierarchical planning and control framework that bridges long-horizon guidance and geometry-aware safety guarantees for a polytopic robot navigating among polytopic obstacles. At the high level, Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is embedded within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework to generate a nominal trajectory around polytopic obstacles while modeling the robot as a point mass for computational tractability. At the low level, we employ a control barrier function (CBF) based on the exact signed distance in the Minkowski-difference space as a safety filter to explicitly enforce the geometric constraints of the robot shape, and further extend its formulation to a high-order CBF (HOCBF). We demonstrate the proposed framework in U-shaped and maze-like environments under single- and double-integrator dynamics. The results show that the proposed architecture mitigates the topology-induced local-minimum behavior of purely reactive CBF-based navigation while enabling safe, real-time, geometry-aware navigation.

</details>


### [115] [Neural-Assisted in-Motion Self-Heading Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00168)
*Zeev Yampolsky,Felipe O. Silva,Adriano Frutuoso,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于神经网络的端到端无模型框架，用于快速准确估计自主海洋平台的初始航向，相比传统模型方法显著提升精度并大幅缩短对准时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主海洋平台需要精确导航，初始航向估计的准确性和所需时间对任务成功至关重要。传统基于模型的航向分解方法（如双矢量分解和优化姿态分解）需要较长的对准时间才能达到满意的精度。

Method: 提出端到端、无模型、神经网络辅助的框架，使用与基于模型方法相同的输入。该方法在自主水面车辆采集的真实世界数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: 相比基于模型的方法，平均绝对误差改善了53%，对准时间最多减少了67%。

Conclusion: 通过提出的方法，减少对准时间和提高精度可以缩短自主平台的部署时间，并在任务期间提高导航精度。

Abstract: Autonomous platforms operating in the oceans require accurate navigation to successfully complete their mission. In this regard, the initial heading estimation accuracy and the time required to achieve it play a critical role. The initial heading is traditionally estimated by model-based approaches employing orientation decomposition. However, methods such as the dual vector decomposition and optimized attitude decomposition achieve satisfactory heading accuracy only after long alignment times. To allow rapid and accurate initial heading estimation, we propose an end-to-end, model-free, neural-assisted framework using the same inputs as the model-based approaches. Our proposed approach was trained and evaluated on real-world dataset captured by an autonomous surface vehicle. Our approach shows a significant accuracy improvement over the model-based approaches achieving an average absolute error improvement of 53%. Additionally, our proposed approach was able to reduce the alignment time by up to 67%. Thus, by employing our proposed approach, the reduction in alignment time and improved accuracy allow for a shorter deployment time of an autonomous platform and increased navigation accuracy during the mission.

</details>


### [116] [DreamControl-v2: Simpler and Scalable Autonomous Humanoid Skills via Trainable Guided Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00202)
*Sudarshan Harithas,Sangkyung Kwak,Pushkal Katara,Srujan Deolasee,Dvij Kalaria,Srinath Sridhar,Sai Vemprala,Ashish Kapoor,Jonathan Chung-Kuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出改进的DreamControl框架，直接在仿人机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型，整合人类和机器人数据集，实现更自动化的技能学习流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人的自主运动操作技能开发仍具挑战性。虽然RL在腿部运动方面成功，但应用于复杂、交互丰富的操作任务时面临长时程规划困难。现有DreamControl方法使用人类运动扩散模型作为生成先验，但存在局限性

Method: 1. 直接在仿人机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型；2. 整合多样化的人类和机器人数据集到统一的体现空间；3. 扩展参考轨迹生成规模以增强下游RL策略鲁棒性；4. 消除手动筛选干预需求，建立更自动化流程

Result: 1. 由于更大的训练数据混合，能够捕捉更广泛的技能范围；2. 建立了更自动化的流程；3. 扩展参考轨迹生成对实现鲁棒下游RL策略至关重要；4. 在仿真和真实Unitree-G1机器人上进行了广泛实验验证

Conclusion: 提出的改进框架通过直接在机器人运动空间训练扩散模型，整合多源数据，实现了更有效和自动化的仿人机器人运动操作技能学习，为复杂交互任务提供了更好的解决方案

Abstract: Developing robust autonomous loco-manipulation skills for humanoids remains an open problem in robotics. While RL has been applied successfully to legged locomotion, applying it to complex, interaction-rich manipulation tasks is harder given long-horizon planning challenges for manipulation. A recent approach along these lines is DreamControl, which addresses these issues by leveraging off-the-shelf human motion diffusion models as a generative prior to guide RL policies during training. In this paper, we investigate the impact of DreamControl's motion prior and propose an improved framework that trains a guided diffusion model directly in the humanoid robot's motion space, aggregating diverse human and robot datasets into a unified embodiment space. We demonstrate that our approach captures a wider range of skills due to the larger training data mixture and establishes a more automated pipeline by removing the need for manual filtering interventions. Furthermore, we show that scaling the generation of reference trajectories is important for achieving robust downstream RL policies. We validate our approach through extensive experiments in simulation and on a real Unitree-G1.

</details>


### [117] [Play-Testing REMind: Evaluating an Educational Robot-Mediated Role-Play Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00300)
*Elaheh Sanoubari,Neil Fernandes,Keith Rebello,Alicia Pan,Andrew Houston,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REMind是一个创新的教育机器人角色扮演游戏，通过机器人中介的戏剧形式支持儿童反欺凌旁观者干预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种创新的教育工具来支持儿童反欺凌旁观者干预，利用机器人技术增强社会情感学习效果。

Method: 设计机器人中介角色扮演游戏(REMind)，让儿童观察机器人表演的欺凌场景，反思角色视角，并通过操控机器人化身练习防御策略。采用混合方法对18名9-10岁儿童进行游戏测试研究。

Result: 研究发现该体验支持了关键学习目标：自我效能感、观点采择、理解防御结果和干预策略。结果显示了机器人中介应用戏剧(RMAD)作为社会情感学习新教学框架的潜力。

Conclusion: REMind展示了机器人中介应用戏剧(RMAD)作为支持社会情感学习的新型教学框架的潜力，为儿童反欺凌教育提供了创新的技术增强方法。

Abstract: This paper presents REMind, an innovative educational robot-mediated role-play game designed to support anti-bullying bystander intervention among children. REMind invites players to observe a bullying scenario enacted by social robots, reflect on the perspectives of the characters, and rehearse defending strategies by puppeteering a robotic avatar. We evaluated REMind through a mixed-methods play-testing study with 18 children aged 9--10. The findings suggest that the experience supported key learning goals related to self-efficacy, perspective-taking, understanding outcomes of defending, and intervention strategies. These results highlight the promise of Robot-Mediated Applied Drama (RMAD) as a novel pedagogical framework to support Social-Emotional Learning.

</details>


### [118] [Hierarchical Motion Planning and Control under Unknown Nonlinear Dynamics via Predicted Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00320)
*Zhiquan Zhang,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划-控制框架，在未知非线性动力学下在线合成运动，通过自适应状态空间分区、图抽象和松弛可达性条件实现探索与利用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知非线性动力学下进行自主运动规划需要同时学习系统特性并导航至目标，现有方法通常需要大量先验知识或计算复杂度过高。

Method: 1) 自适应状态空间分区：将状态空间划分为多面体，在任务相关区域细化分区；2) 分段仿射模型：用PWA模型近似未知非线性系统；3) 图抽象：将PWA系统抽象为有向加权图，边存在性通过可达控制理论和预测可达性条件验证；4) 分层规划：高层图搜索规划，低层仿射反馈控制执行；5) 松弛可达性条件：扩展框架至欠驱动系统。

Result: 仿真表明该方法能有效平衡探索与利用，同时提供形式化的可达性保证，适用于欠驱动系统。

Conclusion: 该框架在有限先验知识下实现了在线运动合成，通过自适应分区、图抽象和松弛可达性条件，为未知非线性动力学系统的自主规划提供了可证明的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous motion planning under unknown nonlinear dynamics requires learning system properties while navigating toward a target. In this work, we develop a hierarchical planning-control framework that enables online motion synthesis with limited prior system knowledge. The state space is partitioned into polytopes and approximates the unknown nonlinear system using a piecewise-affine (PWA) model. The local affine models are identified once the agent enters the corresponding polytopes. To reduce computational complexity, we introduce a non-uniform adaptive state space partition strategy that refines the partition only in task-relevant regions. The resulting PWA system is abstracted into a directed weighted graph, whose edge existence is incrementally verified using reach control theory and predictive reachability conditions. Certified edges are weighted using provable time-to-reach bounds, while uncertain edges are assigned information-theoretic weights to guide exploration. The graph is updated online as new data becomes available, and high-level planning is performed by graph search, while low-level affine feedback controllers are synthesized to execute the plan. Furthermore, the conditions of classical reach control theory are often difficult to satisfy in underactuated settings. We therefore introduce relaxed reachability conditions to extend the framework to such systems. Simulations demonstrate effective exploration-exploitation trade-offs with formal reachability guarantees.

</details>


### [119] [Real Time Local Wind Inference for Robust Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00343)
*Spencer Folk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 无人机通过机载传感器实时推理周围风场，融合地形测量与稀疏风测量预测流场，提升城市环境中自主导航的能效与避障能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个基本问题：1) 地形数据是否足以在密集城市环境中准确预测风场；2) 学习到的风模型如何用于运动规划，重点关注能效和避障

Method: 融合深度学习、流体力学和最优控制工具，建立基于导航LiDAR的局部风预测框架，并将局部风模型先验集成到滚动时域最优控制器中

Result: 在多样化城市风场景模拟中评估风预测器的预测能力，量化局部风信息集成到运动规划后对碰撞率和能耗的改进；亚尺度自由飞行实验证明算法可在嵌入式飞行计算机上实时运行

Conclusion: 提出了一种在未知风环境中进行局部风推理和运动规划的新范式，使机器人能够快速评估局部风况而无需先验环境知识，推动空中机器人进入更具挑战性的环境

Abstract: This thesis presents a solution that enables aerial robots to reason about surrounding wind flow fields in real time using on board sensors and embedded flight hardware. The core novelty of this research is the fusion of range measurements with sparse in situ wind measurements to predict surrounding flow fields. We aim to address two fundamental questions: first, the sufficiency of topographical data for accurate wind prediction in dense urban environments; and second, the utility of learned wind models for motion planning with an emphasis on energy efficiency and obstacle avoidance. Drawing on tools from deep learning, fluid mechanics, and optimal control, we establish a framework for local wind prediction using navigational LiDAR, and then incorporate local wind model priors into a receding-horizon optimal controller to study how local wind knowledge affects energy use and robustness during autonomous navigation. Through simulated demonstrations in diverse urban wind scenarios we evaluate the predictive capabilities of the wind predictor, and quantify improvements to autonomous urban navigation in terms of crash rates and energy consumption when local wind information is integrated into the motion planning. Sub-scale free flight experiments in an open-air wind tunnel demonstrate that these algorithms can run in real time on an embedded flight computer with sufficient bandwidth for stable control of a small aerial robot. Philosophically, this thesis contributes a new paradigm for localized wind inference and motion planning in unknown windy environments. By enabling robots to rapidly assess local wind conditions without prior environmental knowledge, this research accelerates the introduction of aerial robots into increasingly challenging environments.

</details>


### [120] [Go Big or Go Home: Simulating Mobbing Behavior with Braitenbergian Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00350)
*Elaheh Sanoubari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用Webots机器人仿真平台模拟了Braitenberg机器人的群体捕食者规避和围攻行为，研究围攻呼叫范围和群体规模对围攻成功率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿动物界的围攻行为（一种反捕食者适应策略），探索如何通过围攻呼叫协调机器人群体对抗威胁，为人工生命中的行动选择仿真和自主智能体控制架构设计提供参考。

Method: 使用Webots机器人仿真平台，模拟Braitenberg机器人群体面对光源（代表无生命捕食者）时的行为：当遇到威胁时，机器人会发出围攻呼叫召集同伴，如果能召集到盟友就围攻，否则逃跑。研究了围攻呼叫范围（无限、中程、短程）和群体规模（10个机器人vs 3个机器人）两个变量。

Result: 结果表明，围攻呼叫范围和群体规模都对围攻成功率有显著影响。具体来说，更大的呼叫范围和更大的群体规模都能提高围攻的成功率。

Conclusion: 这项研究对人工生命中的行动选择仿真和自主智能体控制架构设计具有重要意义，展示了生物启发机制在机器人群体协调中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We used the Webots robotics simulation platform to simulate a dyadic avoiding and mobbing predator behavior in a group of Braitenbergian robots. Mobbing is an antipredator adaptation used by some animals in which the individuals cooperatively attack or harass a predator to protect themselves. One way of coordinating a mobbing attack is using mobbing calls to summon other individuals of the mobbing species. We imitated this mechanism and simulated Braitenbergian robots that use mobbing calls when they face a light source (representing an inanimate predator) and mob it if they can summon allies, otherwise, they escape from it. We explore the effects of range of mobbing call (infinite range, mid-range and low-range) and the size of the robot group (ten robots vs three) on the overall success of mobbing. Our results suggest that both variables have significant impacts. This work has implications for simulations of action selection in artificial life and designing control architectures for autonomous agents.

</details>


### [121] [A Dual-Stream Transformer Architecture for Illumination-Invariant TIR-LiDAR Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00363)
*Yuki Minase,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种热红外与深度（TIR-D）融合的跟踪架构，通过序列知识迁移策略解决多模态数据稀缺问题，实现全天候鲁棒的人员跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D跟踪在极端光照条件下（如全黑暗或强背光）性能严重下降，需要一种能在全天候条件下鲁棒运行的人员跟踪方案，利用SLAM机器人标配的LiDAR和热红外相机传感器。

Method: 提出TIR-D跟踪架构，采用序列知识迁移策略将大规模热红外预训练模型的结构先验迁移到TIR-D域，使用"细粒度差分学习率策略"保持预训练特征提取能力的同时快速适应几何深度线索。

Result: TIR-D跟踪器在实验中表现出色，平均重叠率（AO）达到0.700，成功率（SR）达到58.7%，显著优于传统的RGB迁移方法和单模态基线。

Conclusion: 该方法为全天候机器人应用中鲁棒的人员跟随提供了实用且资源高效的解决方案，解决了极端光照条件下的跟踪挑战。

Abstract: Robust person tracking is a critical capability for autonomous mobile robots operating in diverse and unpredictable environments. While RGB-D tracking has shown high precision, its performance severely degrades under challenging illumination conditions, such as total darkness or intense backlighting. To achieve all-weather robustness, this paper proposes a novel Thermal-Infrared and Depth (TIR-D) tracking architecture that leverages the standard sensor suite of SLAM-capable robots, namely LiDAR and TIR cameras. A major challenge in TIR-D tracking is the scarcity of annotated multi-modal datasets. To address this, we introduce a sequential knowledge transfer strategy that evolves structural priors from a large-scale thermal-trained model into the TIR-D domain. By employing a differential learning rate strategy -- referred to as ``Fine-grained Differential Learning Rate Strategy'' -- we effectively preserve pre-trained feature extraction capabilities while enabling rapid adaptation to geometric depth cues. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed TIR-D tracker achieves superior performance, with an Average Overlap (AO) of 0.700 and a Success Rate (SR) of 58.7\%, significantly outperforming conventional RGB-transfer and single-modality baselines. Our approach provides a practical and resource-efficient solution for robust human-following in all-weather robotics applications.

</details>


### [122] [Behavioral Score Diffusion: Model-Free Trajectory Planning via Kernel-Based Score Estimation from Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00391)
*Shihao Li,Jiachen Li,Jiamin Xu,Dongmei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Behavioral Score Diffusion (BSD)，一种无需训练、无需模型的轨迹规划方法，通过核加权估计直接从轨迹数据计算扩散分数函数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹优化方法需要在大数据集上训练学习分数网络或使用分析动力学模型进行分数计算，这限制了其应用范围

Method: BSD使用三重核加权方案（扩散邻近性、状态上下文、目标相关性）检索相关轨迹，通过Nadaraya-Watson估计计算去噪轨迹。扩散噪声调度自然控制核带宽，实现多尺度非参数回归

Result: 在四个机器人系统（3D-6D状态空间）的停车场景中，BSD使用固定带宽达到模型基线的98.5%平均奖励，仅需1,000条预收集轨迹，显著优于最近邻检索方法（18-63%改进）

Conclusion: BSD提供了一种无需训练、无需模型的轨迹规划方法，通过核加权估计直接从轨迹数据计算扩散分数，在复杂非线性系统中表现出色，证实扩散去噪机制对数据驱动规划至关重要

Abstract: Diffusion-based trajectory optimization has emerged as a powerful planning paradigm, but existing methods require either learned score networks trained on large datasets or analytical dynamics models for score computation. We introduce \emph{Behavioral Score Diffusion} (BSD), a training-free and model-free trajectory planner that computes the diffusion score function directly from a library of trajectory data via kernel-weighted estimation. At each denoising step, BSD retrieves relevant trajectories using a triple-kernel weighting scheme -- diffusion proximity, state context, and goal relevance -- and computes a Nadaraya-Watson estimate of the denoised trajectory. The diffusion noise schedule naturally controls kernel bandwidths, creating a multi-scale nonparametric regression: broad averaging of global behavioral patterns at high noise, fine-grained local interpolation at low noise. This coarse-to-fine structure handles nonlinear dynamics without linearization or parametric assumptions. Safety is preserved by applying shielded rollout on kernel-estimated state trajectories, identical to existing model-based approaches. We evaluate BSD on four robotic systems of increasing complexity (3D--6D state spaces) in a parking scenario. BSD with fixed bandwidth achieves 98.5\% of the model-based baseline's average reward across systems while requiring no dynamics model, using only 1{,}000 pre-collected trajectories. BSD substantially outperforms nearest-neighbor retrieval (18--63\% improvement), confirming that the diffusion denoising mechanism is essential for effective data-driven planning.

</details>


### [123] [Sampling-based Task and Kinodynamic Motion Planning under Semantic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00401)
*Qi Heng Ho,Zachary N. Sunberg,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于不确定环境中集成任务和动力学运动规划的算法，结合决策技术和采样运动规划，证明算法正确性和渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不确定环境中的集成任务和动力学运动规划问题。机器人具有非线性动力学，需要在部分可观测环境中执行有限迹线性时序逻辑（LTLf）规范，环境不确定性体现在语义标签上。

Method: 将问题建模为部分可观测随机混合系统，捕获机器人动力学、LTLf任务和环境状态变量不确定性。提出一种利用混合系统结构的随时算法，结合决策技术和采样运动规划。

Result: 证明了算法的正确性和渐近最优性。实验结果显示算法在不确定环境中有效，且始终优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的算法成功解决了不确定环境中的集成任务和动力学运动规划问题，结合决策和运动规划技术，在理论和实验上都表现出色。

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of integrated task and kinodynamic motion planning in uncertain environments. We consider a robot with nonlinear dynamics tasked with a Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\ltlf$) specification operating in a partially observable environment. Specifically, the uncertainty is in the semantic labels of the environment. We show how the problem can be modeled as a Partially Observable Stochastic Hybrid System that captures the robot dynamics, $\ltlf$ task, and uncertainty in the environment state variables. We propose an anytime algorithm that takes advantage of the structure of the hybrid system, and combines the effectiveness of decision-making techniques and sampling-based motion planning. We prove the soundness and asymptotic optimality of the algorithm. Results show the efficacy of our algorithm in uncertain environments, and that it consistently outperforms baseline methods.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Humanoid Navigation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00416)
*Weizhuo Wang,Yanjie Ze,C. Karen Liu,Monroe Kennedy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav系统仅用5小时人类行走数据训练，无需机器人数据或微调，就能让仿人机器人在未见环境中导航。系统使用扩散模型预测未来轨迹分布，结合360度视觉记忆和DINOv3特征，通过混合采样实现实时推理，在真实环境中表现出自然避障和社交行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人导航系统通常需要大量机器人特定数据或复杂微调，限制了在多样未见环境中的泛化能力。本文旨在开发一个仅从少量人类行走数据学习就能让仿人机器人零样本适应各种环境的系统。

Method: 1. 扩散模型预测未来轨迹分布，条件包括过去轨迹、360度视觉记忆（融合颜色、深度和语义）以及DINOv3视频特征；2. 混合采样方案实现10步去噪的实时推理；3. 滚动时域控制器从预测分布中选择路径。

Result: 离线评估显示EgoNav在避障和多模态覆盖方面优于基线。在Unitree G1仿人机器人上的零样本部署中，系统能在未见室内外环境中自然实现等待开门、绕开人群、避开玻璃墙等行为。

Conclusion: 仅用5小时人类数据训练的EgoNav系统实现了仿人机器人在多样未见环境中的零样本导航，无需机器人数据或微调，展示了从人类先验中学习复杂导航行为的可行性。

Abstract: We present EgoNav, a system that enables a humanoid robot to traverse diverse, unseen environments by learning entirely from 5 hours of human walking data, with no robot data or finetuning. A diffusion model predicts distributions of plausible future trajectories conditioned on past trajectory, a 360 deg visual memory fusing color, depth, and semantics, and video features from a frozen DINOv3 backbone that capture appearance cues invisible to depth sensors. A hybrid sampling scheme achieves real-time inference in 10 denoising steps, and a receding-horizon controller selects paths from the predicted distribution. We validate EgoNav through offline evaluations, where it outperforms baselines in collision avoidance and multi-modal coverage, and through zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid across unseen indoor and outdoor environments. Behaviors such as waiting for doors to open, navigating around crowds, and avoiding glass walls emerge naturally from the learned prior. We will release the dataset and trained models. Our website: https://egonav.weizhuowang.com

</details>


### [125] [Certificate-Driven Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding with Inheritable Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00428)
*Jiarui Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CDCBS通过引入证书轨迹和舰队预算机制，改进了ACCBS在密集场景下的短视问题，提高了解决方案质量的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环MAPF算法（如ACCBS）在有限计算预算下可能产生短视的解决方案，特别是在密集场景中难以保持全局保证和利用组合结构。

Method: 提出证书轨迹和舰队预算机制作为通用过滤框架，证书提供无冲突回退计划和剩余成本单调上界，预算信息诱导预算限制分解，实现跨时间步的全局可继承分解。

Result: 在基准地图上的实验表明，CDCBS比ACCBS获得更一致的解决方案质量，特别是在密集设置中，同时提出的分解机制减少了有效组大小。

Conclusion: 证书驱动方法为闭环MAPF算法提供了系统性的改进框架，通过证书轨迹和预算分解机制解决了有限视野下的短视问题，提高了算法的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination in automated warehouses and logistics is commonly modeled as the Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) problem. Closed-loop MAPF algorithms improve scalability by planning only the next movement and replanning online, but this finite-horizon viewpoint can be shortsighted and makes it difficult to preserve global guarantees and exploit compositional structure. This issue is especially visible in Anytime Closed-Loop Conflict-Based Search (ACCBS), which applies Conflict-Based Search (CBS) over dynamically extended finite horizons but, under finite computational budgets, may terminate with short active prefixes in dense instances.
  We introduce certificate trajectories and their associated fleet budget as a general mechanism for filtering closed-loop updates. A certificate provides a conflict-free fallback plan and a monotone upper bound on the remaining cost; accepting only certificate-improving updates yields completeness. The same budget information induces a budget-limited factorization that enables global, inheritable decomposition across timesteps. Instantiating the framework on ACCBS yields Certificate-Driven Conflict-Based Search (CDCBS). Experiments on benchmark maps show that CDCBS achieves more consistent solution quality than ACCBS, particularly in dense settings, while the proposed factorization reduces effective group size.

</details>


### [126] [Reachability-Aware Time Scaling for Path Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00439)
*Hossein Gholampour,Logan E. Beaver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于双积分器系统的路径跟踪方法，结合离线速度缩放和在线QP跟踪，确保在加速度限制下安全跟踪包含急转弯的高曲率路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的规划器生成的路径可能包含急转弯和高曲率区域，即使在几何上无碰撞，加速度限制下的单步可达性也变得至关重要，需要确保系统能够安全跟踪这些路径

Method: 采用纯追踪风格的、可达性引导的二次规划跟踪器，结合单步加速度裕度。离线阶段：沿路径样条评估加速度裕度，更新标量速度缩放剖面。在线阶段：使用相同的向前看跟踪结构跟踪缩放后的参考路径

Result: 该方法能够在加速度限制下安全跟踪包含急转弯和高曲率区域的路径，确保系统在跟踪过程中保持单步可达性，避免违反加速度约束

Conclusion: 通过结合离线速度缩放和在线QP跟踪，提出了一种有效的路径跟踪方法，能够处理基于采样规划器生成的复杂路径，在加速度约束下保证跟踪安全性

Abstract: This paper studies tracking of collision-free waypoint paths produced by an offline planner for a planar double-integrator system with bounded speed and acceleration. Because sampling-based planners must route around obstacles, the resulting waypoint paths can contain sharp turns and high-curvature regions, so one-step reachability under acceleration limits becomes critical even when the path geometry is collision-free. We build on a pure-pursuit-style, reachability-guided quadratic-program (QP) tracker with a one-step acceleration margin. Offline, we evaluate this margin along a spline fitted to the waypoint path and update a scalar speed-scaling profile so that the required one-step acceleration remains below the available bound. Online, the same look-ahead tracking structure is used to track the scaled reference.

</details>


### [127] [Multi-Camera View Scaling for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00557)
*Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Shuqi Zhao,Cheng-En Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianwen Xie,Hao-Shu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过多视角相机采集专家演示，无需额外人工成本即可增强模仿学习的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的泛化能力受限于专家演示的多样性，而收集多样化环境的演示成本高且困难

Method: 使用多个同步相机视角从每个专家轨迹生成伪演示，增强训练分布；分析不同动作空间与视角扩展的交互；引入多视角动作聚合方法

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，相比单视角基线显著提升了数据效率和泛化能力

Conclusion: 扩展相机视角为模仿学习提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，只需最小额外硬件设置，并能无缝集成到现有算法中

Abstract: The generalization ability of imitation learning policies for robotic manipulation is fundamentally constrained by the diversity of expert demonstrations, while collecting demonstrations across varied environments is costly and difficult in practice. In this paper, we propose a practical framework that exploits inherent scene diversity without additional human effort by scaling camera views during demonstration collection. Instead of acquiring more trajectories, multiple synchronized camera perspectives are used to generate pseudo-demonstrations from each expert trajectory, which enriches the training distribution and improves viewpoint invariance in visual representations. We analyze how different action spaces interact with view scaling and show that camera-space representations further enhance diversity. In addition, we introduce a multiview action aggregation method that allows single-view policies to benefit from multiple cameras during deployment. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate significant gains in data efficiency and generalization compared to single-view baselines. Our results suggest that scaling camera views provides a practical and scalable solution for imitation learning, which requires minimal additional hardware setup and integrates seamlessly with existing imitation learning algorithms. The website of our project is https://yichen928.github.io/robot_multiview.

</details>


### [128] [A Physical Imitation Learning Pipeline for Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion Assisted by Parallel Elastic Joint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00611)
*Huyue Ma,Yurui Jin,Helmut Hauser,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出物理模仿学习（PIL）方法，将强化学习控制策略蒸馏到可物理实现的被动并联弹性关节中，让机器人身体模仿部分控制行为，从而降低能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物通过脑体协同进化，利用内在身体动力学实现节能运动，而机器人通常采用集中控制器抑制而非利用身体动力学。需要开发能利用身体被动特性的方法来实现具身物理智能

Method: 物理模仿学习（PIL）：将强化学习控制策略蒸馏到可物理实现的被动并联弹性关节（PEJs）中，让身体模仿部分控制行为，剩余部分由电机执行，从而降低整体能耗

Result: 在模拟四足机器人中，PIL方法能在平坦地形上将87%的机械功率卸载到PEJs，在崎岖地形上卸载18%，实现显著节能效果

Conclusion: PIL通过从控制策略中蒸馏而非联合优化身体设计，实现了脑体协同设计而不扩展搜索空间，为广泛关节型机器人形态提供了计算高效的任务特定具身物理智能实现途径

Abstract: Due to brain-body co-evolution, animals' intrinsic body dynamics play a crucial role in energy-efficient locomotion, which shares control effort between active muscles and passive body dynamics -- a principle known as Embodied Physical Intelligence. In contrast, robot bodies are often designed with one centralised controller that typically suppress the intrinsic body dynamics instead of exploiting it. We introduce Physical Imitation Learning (PIL), which distils a Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy into physically implementable body responses that can be directly offloaded to passive Parallel Elastic Joints (PEJs), enabling therefore the body to imitate part of the controlled behaviour. Meanwhile, the residual policy commands the motors to recover the RL policy's performance. The results is an overall reduced energy consumption thanks to outsourcing parts of the control policy to the PEJs. Here we show in simulated quadrupeds, that our PIL approach can offloads up to 87% of mechanical power to PEJs on flat terrain and 18% on rough terrain. Because the body design is distilled from -- rather than jointly optimised with -- the control policy, PIL realises brain-body co-design without expanding the search space with body design parameters, providing a computationally efficient route to task-specific Embodied Physical Intelligence applicable to a wide range of joint-based robot morphologies.

</details>


### [129] [StretchBot: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Adaptive Guidance with Assistive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00628)
*Luca Vogelgesang,Ahmed Mehdi Soltani,Mohammadhossein Khojasteh,Xinrui Zu,Stefano De Giorgis,Madalina Croitoru,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StretchBot是一个混合神经符号机器人教练，结合多模态感知和知识图谱增强的大语言模型推理，提供自适应拉伸指导，相比脚本化系统能更好地适应用户状态和环境上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前辅助机器人系统大多采用脚本化设计，无法根据用户状态、环境上下文和交互动态进行自适应调整，限制了其在家庭和医疗场景中的实际应用效果。

Method: 开发了StretchBot混合神经符号机器人教练系统，结合多模态感知与知识图谱增强的大语言模型推理，支持在保持结构化常规的同时进行上下文感知的自适应调整。

Result: 探索性试点比较显示，自适应条件在感知适应性和上下文相关性方面表现更好，而脚本化指导在流畅性和可预测性方面仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构化可操作知识有助于在具身辅助交互中基于语言模型进行自适应，但需要更大规模、纵向研究来评估系统的鲁棒性、泛化能力和长期用户体验。

Abstract: Assistive robots have growing potential to support physical wellbeing in home and healthcare settings, for example, by guiding users through stretching or rehabilitation routines. However, existing systems remain largely scripted, which limits their ability to adapt to user state, environmental context, and interaction dynamics. In this work, we present StretchBot, a hybrid neuro-symbolic robotic coach for adaptive assistive guidance. The system combines multimodal perception with knowledge-graph-grounded large language model reasoning to support context-aware adjustments during short stretching sessions while maintaining a structured routine. To complement the system description, we report an exploratory pilot comparison between scripted and adaptive guidance with three participants. The pilot findings suggest that the adaptive condition improved perceived adaptability and contextual relevance, while scripted guidance remained competitive in smoothness and predictability. These results provide preliminary evidence that structured actionable knowledge can help ground language-model-based adaptation in embodied assistive interaction, while also highlighting the need for larger, longitudinal studies to evaluate robustness, generalizability, and long-term user experience.

</details>


### [130] [LiPS: Lightweight Panoptic Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00634)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiPS：一种轻量级全景分割方法，在保持查询式解码的同时简化特征提取与融合，实现高效计算，适合移动机器人等资源受限平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的全景分割模型过于复杂，不适合部署在移动机器人等资源受限平台上。需要一种既能保持高性能又能大幅降低计算需求的方法。

Method: 提出LiPS方法，采用轻量级设计，保留基于查询的解码机制，同时引入简化的特征提取和融合路径。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，LiPS达到与更重基线相当的精度，同时提供高达4.5倍的吞吐量（每秒帧数），计算量减少近6.8倍。

Conclusion: LiPS为现代全景分割模型与现实世界机器人应用之间搭建了高效桥梁，使高质量全景分割在资源受限平台上成为可能。

Abstract: Panoptic segmentation is a key enabler for robotic perception, as it unifies semantic understanding with object-level reasoning. However, the increasing complexity of state-of-the-art models makes them unsuitable for deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as mobile robots. We propose a novel approach called LiPS that addresses the challenge of efficient-to-compute panoptic segmentation with a lightweight design that retains query-based decoding while introducing a streamlined feature extraction and fusion pathway. It aims at providing a strong panoptic segmentation performance while substantially lowering the computational demands. Evaluations on standard benchmarks demonstrate that LiPS attains accuracy comparable to much heavier baselines, while providing up to 4.5 higher throughput, measured in frames per second, and requiring nearly 6.8 times fewer computations. This efficiency makes LiPS a highly relevant bridge between modern panoptic models and real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [131] [SoftHand Model-W: A 3D-Printed, Anthropomorphic, Underactuated Robot Hand with Integrated Wrist and Carpal Tunnel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00738)
*Dhillon B. Merritt,Christopher J. Ford,Haoran Li,Malia Smith,Zhixing Chen,Efi Psomopoulou,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftHand Model-W是一款3D打印的欠驱动仿人机器人手，集成了拮抗肌腱机制和2自由度肌腱驱动腕部，通过腕部主动运动减少手臂补偿动作，提高任务完成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有腕部主动自由度的机器人手，以减少手臂的补偿性运动，实现更高效、更类人的操作能力，特别是在需要手-臂协调的任务中。

Method: 基于Pisa/IIT SoftHand设计，采用3D打印制造，集成拮抗肌腱机制和2自由度肌腱驱动腕部（屈伸和桡尺偏），通过腕管式肌腱布线将电机放置在远端前臂，减少末端惯性。

Result: 在立方体堆叠和平面圆盘旋转任务中，腕部驱动显著减少了任务完成时间、手臂关节行程和配置变化，并实现了原本不可能的拾放操作。

Conclusion: 近端自由度（如腕部）是实现多功能、类人操作的关键，紧凑设计使其适用于研究和辅助应用场景。

Abstract: This paper presents the SoftHand Model-W: a 3D-printed, underactuated, anthropomorphic robot hand based on the Pisa/IIT SoftHand, with an integrated antagonistic tendon mechanism and 2 degree-of-freedom tendon-driven wrist. These four degrees-of-acuation provide active flexion and extension to the five fingers, and active flexion/extension and radial/ulnar deviation of the palm through the wrist, while preserving the synergistic and self-adaptive features of such SoftHands. A carpal tunnel-inspired tendon routing allows remote motor placement in the forearm, reducing distal inertia and maintaining a compact form factor. The SoftHand-W is mounted on a 6-axis robot arm and tested with two reorientation tasks requiring coordination between the hand and arm's pose: cube stacking and in-plane disc rotation. Results comparing task time, arm joint travel, and configuration changes with and without wrist actuation show that adding the wrist reduces compensatory and reconfiguration movements of the arm for a quicker task-completion time. Moreover, the wrist enables pick-and-place operations that would be impossible otherwise. Overall, the SoftHand Model-W demonstrates how proximal degrees of freedom are key to achieving versatile, human-like manipulation in real world robotic applications, with a compact design enabling deployment in research and assistive settings.

</details>


### [132] [How to Train your Tactile Model: Tactile Perception with Multi-fingered Robot Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00744)
*Christopher J. Ford,Kaichen Shi,Laura Butcher,Nathan F. Lepora,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacViT：基于Vision Transformer的新型触觉感知模型，通过全局自注意力机制从触觉图像中提取鲁棒特征，能够在新传感器上实现泛化，减少数据收集和重新训练需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于CNN的触觉感知方法严重依赖传感器特定的大数据集，且由于镜头特性、照明条件和传感器磨损等差异，每个新传感器都需要重新训练，这阻碍了触觉传感器在机器人操作中的快速部署和可扩展性。

Method: 提出TacViT模型，基于Vision Transformers架构，利用全局自注意力机制从触觉图像中提取鲁棒特征，设计用于在新传感器数据上实现泛化。

Result: 在五指机器人手的传感器上评估TacViT，相比CNN展现出更优越的泛化性能，能够准确推断接触属性，即使是在未见过的传感器上。

Conclusion: TacViT显著减少了新传感器部署所需的数据收集和重新训练，加速了触觉传感器的部署，使触觉感知在真实世界机器人应用中更具可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Rapid deployment of new tactile sensors is essential for scalable robotic manipulation, especially in multi-fingered hands equipped with vision-based tactile sensors. However, current methods for inferring contact properties rely heavily on convolutional neural networks (CNNs), which, while effective on known sensors, require large, sensor-specific datasets. Furthermore, they require retraining for each new sensor due to differences in lens properties, illumination, and sensor wear. Here we introduce TacViT, a novel tactile perception model based on Vision Transformers, designed to generalize on new sensor data. TacViT leverages global self-attention mechanisms to extract robust features from tactile images, enabling accurate contact property inference even on previously unseen sensors. This capability significantly reduces the need for data collection and retraining, accelerating the deployment of new sensors. We evaluate TacViT on sensors for a five-fingered robot hand and demonstrate its superior generalization performance compared to CNNs. Our results highlight TacViTs potential to make tactile sensing more scalable and practical for real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [133] [A wearable haptic device for edge and surface simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00752)
*Rui Chen,Xianlong Mai,Alireza Sanaei,Domenico Chiaradia,Antonio Frisoli,Daniele Leonardis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑轻量的指尖触觉设备，通过双电机机制提供可区分的表面和边缘接触反馈，在VR中增强物体操作的真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统指尖触觉设备无法渲染某些对沉浸式和精确交互相关的触觉特征（如边缘检测），限制了VR应用中物体操作的逼真度。

Method: 开发了一种紧凑轻量（24.3克）的指尖触觉设备，采用新颖的双电机机制，通过6×6柔性传感器阵列进行压力分布表征，验证两种刺激模式的不同接触模式。

Result: 初步用户研究（5名参与者）在四种条件（轻/重压力下的边缘/表面接触）下达到93%的平均分类准确率，平均响应时间为2.79秒，表明设备能有效传达边缘和表面触觉线索。

Conclusion: 该设备能够有效传达边缘和表面触觉线索，有望提高VR环境中物体操作的逼真度，为沉浸式VR交互提供更好的触觉反馈解决方案。

Abstract: Object manipulation is fundamental to virtual reality (VR) applications, yet conventional fingertip haptic devices fail to render certain tactile features relevant for immersive and precise interactions, as i.e. detection of edges. This paper presents a compact, lightweight fingertip haptic device (24.3 g) that delivers distinguishable surface and edge contact feedback through a novel dual-motor mechanism. Pressure distribution characterization using a 6 x 6 flexible sensor array demonstrates distinct contact patterns between the two stimulation modes. A preliminary user study with five participants achieved 93% average classification accuracy across four conditions (edge/surface contact with light/heavy pressure), with mean response times of 2.79 seconds. The results indicate that the proposed device can effectively convey edge and surface tactile cues, potentially enhancing object manipulation fidelity in VR environments.

</details>


### [134] [A Dual-Action Fabric-Based Soft Robotic Glove for Ergonomic Hand Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00768)
*Rui Chen,Firman Isma Serdana,Domenico Chiaradia,Xianlong Mai,Elena Losanno,Gabriele Righi,Claudia De Santis,Federica Serra,Vincent Mendez,Cristian Camardella,Daniele Leonardis,Giulio Del Popolo,Silvestro Micera,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于织物的双作用软体机器人手套，具有个性化定制的执行器，支持手指屈伸和拇指外展，通过热封技术制造，能有效辅助手部功能障碍患者的日常生活活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经系统疾病导致的手部功能障碍严重影响日常生活独立性，现有软体机器人手套在定制化、人体工程学适配和屈伸驱动方面存在局限，限制了临床应用。

Method: 采用计算机数控热封技术制造对称腔室执行器，设计具有五个独立控制的双作用执行器（支持手指屈伸）和专门的拇指外展执行器，执行器充气时形成凹形外表面以最大化手指接触面积。

Result: 执行器产生足够的关节力矩和指尖力用于日常生活任务；10名健康受试者测试显示手套显著降低前臂肌肉活动；3名颈脊髓损伤患者试点研究表明手套促进更自然的抓握模式，减少肌腱固定抓握依赖，但任务完成时间增加。

Conclusion: 这种可定制、符合人体工程学的设计代表了向个性化手部康复和辅助机器人迈出的实用一步，尽管当前驱动接口导致任务完成时间增加需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Hand impairment following neurological disorders substantially limits independence in activities of daily living, motivating the development of effective assistive and rehabilitation strategies. Soft robotic gloves have attracted growing interest in this context, yet persistent challenges in customization, ergonomic fit, and flexion-extension actuation constrain their clinical utility. Here, we present a dual-action fabric-based soft robotic glove incorporating customized actuators aligned with individual finger joints. The glove comprises five independently controlled dual-action actuators supporting finger flexion and extension, together with a dedicated thumb abduction actuator. Leveraging computer numerical control heat sealing technology, we fabricated symmetrical-chamber actuators that adopt a concave outer surface upon inflation, thereby maximizing finger contact area and improving comfort. Systematic characterization confirmed that the actuators generate sufficient joint moment and fingertip force for ADL-relevant tasks, and that the complete glove system produces adequate grasping force for common household objects. A preliminary study with ten healthy subjects demonstrated that active glove assistance significantly reduces forearm muscle activity during object manipulation. A pilot feasibility study with three individuals with cervical spinal cord injury across seven functional tasks indicated that glove assistance promotes more natural grasp patterns and reduces reliance on tenodesis grasp, although at the cost of increased task completion time attributable to the current actuation interface. This customizable, ergonomic design represents a practical step toward personalized hand rehabilitation and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [135] [Compact Keyframe-Optimized Multi-Agent Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00804)
*Monica M. Q. Li,Pierre-Yves Lajoie,Jialiang Liu,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出改进的多智能体RGB-D高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过压缩冗余高斯和集中式闭环检测，在保持地图质量的同时减少85-95%的通信负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体3D建图中，密集表示（如高斯泼溅）在有限带宽下实时交换困难，现有集中式SLAM系统在合并本地地图时面临通信瓶颈。

Method: 1) 在SLAM系统中加入压缩步骤，去除冗余3D高斯而不降低渲染质量；2) 采用无需初始猜测的集中式闭环检测，支持纯渲染深度模式（仅需3D高斯）和相机深度模式（包含轻量深度图像以提高配准精度和进一步高斯剪枝）。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上评估，相比最先进方法，两种模式下传输数据减少85-95%，使3D高斯多智能体SLAM更接近实际部署。

Conclusion: 提出的框架显著降低了多智能体高斯泼溅SLAM的通信负载，同时保持地图保真度，为实际场景部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Efficient multi-agent 3D mapping is essential for robotic teams operating in unknown environments, but dense representations hinder real-time exchange over constrained communication links. In multi-agent Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), systems typically rely on a centralized server to merge and optimize the local maps produced by individual agents. However, sharing these large map representations, particularly those generated by recent methods such as Gaussian Splatting, becomes a bottleneck in real-world scenarios with limited bandwidth. We present an improved multi-agent RGB-D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that reduces communication load while preserving map fidelity. First, we incorporate a compaction step into our SLAM system to remove redundant 3D Gaussians, without degrading the rendering quality. Second, our approach performs centralized loop closure computation without initial guess, operating in two modes: a pure rendered-depth mode that requires no data beyond the 3D Gaussians, and a camera-depth mode that includes lightweight depth images for improved registration accuracy and additional Gaussian pruning. Evaluation on both synthetic and real-world datasets shows up to 85-95\% reduction in transmitted data compared to state-of-the-art approaches in both modes, bringing 3D Gaussian multi-agent SLAM closer to practical deployment in real-world scenarios. Code: https://github.com/lemonci/coko-slam

</details>


### [136] [PanoAir: A Panoramic Visual-Inertial SLAM with Cross-Time Real-World UAV Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00852)
*Yiyang Wu,Xiaohu Zhang,Yanjin Du,Tongsu Zhang,Chujun Li,Siyang Chen,Guoyi Zhang,Xiangpeng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个真实世界全景视觉惯性数据集和全景VI-SLAM框架，通过全景特征提取和闭环增强无人机姿态估计的准确性和鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VI-SLAM方法主要依赖有限视场传感器，在复杂无人机场景中容易产生漂移甚至失败。全景相机提供全向感知但相关研究和数据集仍不足

Method: 构建真实世界全景视觉惯性数据集，提出全景VI-SLAM框架，包括全景特征提取和全景闭环检测，利用全向视场增强特征约束和全局一致性

Result: 在提出的数据集和公共基准测试中，该方法在准确性、鲁棒性和一致性方面优于现有方法，在嵌入式平台上实现与PC实现相当的效率

Conclusion: 全景VI-SLAM框架通过利用全向视场显著提升了无人机姿态估计性能，数据集和代码公开促进了该领域研究

Abstract: Accurate pose estimation is fundamental for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) applications, where Visual-Inertial SLAM (VI-SLAM) provides a cost-effective solution for localization and mapping. However, existing VI-SLAM methods mainly rely on sensors with limited fields of view (FoV), which can lead to drift and even failure in complex UAV scenarios. Although panoramic cameras provide omnidirectional perception to improve robustness, panoramic VI-SLAM and corresponding real-world datasets for UAVs remain underexplored. To address this limitation, we first construct a real-world panoramic visual-inertial dataset covering diverse flight conditions, including varying illumination, altitudes, trajectory lengths, and motion dynamics. To achieve accurate and robust pose estimation under such challenging UAV scenarios, we propose a panoramic VI-SLAM framework that exploits the omnidirectional FoV via the proposed panoramic feature extraction and panoramic loop closure, enhancing feature constraints and ensuring global consistency. Extensive experiments on both the proposed dataset and public benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy, robustness, and consistency compared to existing approaches. Moreover, deployment on embedded platform validates its practical applicability, achieving comparable computational efficiency to PC implementations.
  The source code and dataset are publicly available at https://drive.google.com/file/d/1lG1Upn6yi-N6tYpEHAt6dfR1uhzNtWbT/view

</details>


### [137] [An Integrated Soft Robotic System for Measuring Vital Signs in Search and Rescue Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00971)
*Jorge Francisco García-Samartín,Christyan Cruz Ulloa,Andrés Sánchez-Silva,Jaime del Cerro,Antonio Barrientos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种用于灾后救援的软体抓取器，能够测量受害者的脉搏和血压，精度达到脉搏偏差4bpm，血压偏差约5mmHg，并通过实际测试验证了系统功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器人在搜救行动中广泛应用，受害者的生命体征评估技术已有进步，但在脉搏测量方面仍存在问题，且缺乏针对灾后场景的血压评估研究。需要开发能够在灾后环境中准确测量受害者生命体征的设备。

Method: 设计了一种软体抓取器，集成到移动机器人系统中。该抓取器能够包裹受害者手臂并像血压计一样充气，通过专门的便携系统实现。采用不同的信号处理算法来处理测量数据。

Result: 系统实现了脉搏偏差4bpm，收缩压和舒张压偏差约5mmHg的测量精度。统计数据和误差项同方差性验证证明了系统能够准确确定心率和血压。实际灾后测试验证了系统能够适应不同受害者位置，测量速度快且对受害者安全。

Conclusion: 开发的软体抓取器系统能够准确测量灾后受害者的脉搏和血压，适合搜救行动使用。系统具有良好的适应性、测量速度和安全性，为灾后生命体征评估提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Robots are frequently utilized in search-and-rescue operations. In recent years, significant advancements have been made in the field of victim assessment. However, there are still open issues regarding heart rate measurement, and no studies have been found that assess pressure in post-disaster scenarios. This work designs a soft gripper and integrates it into a mobile robotic system, thereby creating a device capable of measuring the pulse and blood pressure of victims in post-disaster environments. The gripper is designed to envelop the victim's arm and inflate like a sphygmomanometer, facilitated by a specialized portability system. The utilization of different signal processing algorithms has enabled the attainment of a pulse bias of \qty{4}{\bpm} and a bias of approximately \qty{5}{\mmHg} for systolic and diastolic pressures. The findings, in conjunction with the other statistical data and the validation of homoscedasticity in the error terms, prove the system's capacity to accurately determine heart rate and blood pressure, thereby rendering it suitable for search and rescue operations. Finally, a post-disaster has been employed as a test to validate the functionality of the entire system and to demonstrate its capacity to adapt to various victim positions, its measurement speed, and its safety for victims.

</details>


### [138] [Infinite-Horizon Ergodic Control via Kernel Mean Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01023)
*Christian Hughes,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于核均值嵌入的无限时域遍历控制器，解决长期覆盖任务中计算复杂度问题，通过扩展状态记录历史访问实现无限时域控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于核的遍历控制方法在一般覆盖域上提供强覆盖保证，但由于计算复杂度随时域增长而急剧增加，实际应用仅限于有限时域，无法用于长期覆盖任务。

Method: 推导无限时域遍历控制器，引入扩展核均值嵌入误差访问状态递归记录状态访问历史，将过去访问与未来控制解耦，扩展到无限时域设置；同时提出基于扩展误差状态的滚动时域控制变体。

Result: 证明了推导控制器的渐近收敛性，并在2D和3D覆盖问题中保持了遍历覆盖保证，解决了长期覆盖任务的计算可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 提出的无限时域遍历控制器成功解决了核基遍历控制在长期覆盖任务中的计算可扩展性限制，通过扩展状态记录访问历史实现了无限时域控制，为一般域上的长期覆盖任务提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper derives an infinite-horizon ergodic controller based on kernel mean embeddings for long-duration coverage tasks on general domains. While existing kernel-based ergodic control methods provide strong coverage guarantees on general coverage domains, their practical use has been limited to sub-ergodic, finite-time horizons due to intractable computational scaling, prohibiting its use for long-duration coverage. We resolve this scaling by deriving an infinite-horizon ergodic controller equipped with an extended kernel mean embedding error visitation state that recursively records state visitation. This extended state decouples past visitation from future control synthesis and expands ergodic control to infinite-time settings. In addition, we present a variation of the controller that operates on a receding-horizon control formulation with the extended error state. We demonstrate theoretical proof of asymptotic convergence of the derived controller and show preservation of ergodic coverage guarantees for a class of 2D and 3D coverage problems.

</details>


### [139] [Stein Variational Uncertainty-Adaptive Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01034)
*Hrishikesh Sathyanarayan,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Stein变分分布鲁棒控制器的非线性动力系统控制方法，用于处理潜在参数不确定性，通过任务相关的粒子近似替代保守的最坏情况优化，在保持计算并行性的同时实现性能与鲁棒性的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布鲁棒优化（DRO）方法在处理非线性动力系统的参数不确定性时，通常采用保守的最坏情况设计，这会牺牲名义性能。需要一种方法能够专注于对闭环性能影响最大的参数敏感性，同时避免对不确定性模型的限制性参数假设。

Method: 将最优控制与Stein变分推理相结合，使用确定性粒子基近似任务相关的概率分布，形成分布鲁棒控制器。该方法不依赖不确定性模型的参数假设，保持计算并行性，通过塑造控制律围绕对任务目标最关键的相关不确定性来实现鲁棒性。

Result: 在代表性控制问题上实证表明，该方法相比名义控制、集成控制和经典分布鲁棒基线方法，实现了更好的性能-鲁棒性权衡，能够在不牺牲名义性能的情况下获得鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将鲁棒控制与变分推理统一在单一决策理论公式中，适用于具有参数不确定性的广泛控制系统类别，为非线性动力系统的鲁棒控制提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: We propose a Stein variational distributionally robust controller for nonlinear dynamical systems with latent parametric uncertainty. The method is an alternative to conservative worst-case ambiguity-set optimization with a deterministic particle-based approximation of a task-dependent uncertainty distribution, enabling the controller to concentrate on parameter sensitivities that most strongly affect closed-loop performance. Our method yields a controller that is robust to latent parameter uncertainty by coupling optimal control with Stein variational inference, and avoiding restrictive parametric assumptions on the uncertainty model while preserving computational parallelism. In contrast to classical DRO, which can sacrifice nominal performance through worst-case design, we find our approach achieves robustness by shaping the control law around relevant uncertainty that are most critical to the task objective. The proposed framework therefore reconciles robust control and variational inference in a single decision-theoretic formulation for broad classes of control systems with parameter uncertainty. We demonstrate our approach on representative control problems that empirically illustrate improved performance-robustness tradeoffs over nominal, ensemble, and classical distributionally robust baselines.

</details>


### [140] [BAT: Balancing Agility and Stability via Online Policy Switching for Long-Horizon Whole-Body Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01064)
*Donghoon Baek,Sang-Hun Kim,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BAT框架通过在线策略切换，动态选择互补的全身RL控制器，在长时程任务中平衡敏捷性与稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在全局协调的耦合全身策略和模块化精度的解耦策略之间存在权衡，缺乏系统整合方法来解决敏捷性、鲁棒性和精度之间的平衡问题

Method: 提出BAT在线策略切换框架，包含两个互补模块：1）通过分层RL学习的切换策略，带有滑动时域策略预评估的专家指导；2）选项感知的VQ-VAE，从离散运动令牌序列预测选项偏好以提升泛化能力。最终决策通过两个模块的置信度加权融合获得

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，BAT能够实现多功能的长时程移动操作，并在多样化任务中优于先前方法

Conclusion: BAT框架通过动态策略切换成功解决了敏捷性、鲁棒性和精度之间的权衡问题，为人形机器人实现多功能长时程任务提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Despite recent advances in control, reinforcement learning, and imitation learning, developing a unified framework that can achieve agile, precise, and robust whole-body behaviors, particularly in long-horizon tasks, remains challenging. Existing approaches typically follow two paradigms: coupled whole-body policies for global coordination and decoupled policies for modular precision. However, without a systematic method to integrate both, this trade-off between agility, robustness, and precision remains unresolved. In this work, we propose BAT, an online policy-switching framework that dynamically selects between two complementary whole-body RL controllers to balance agility and stability across different motion contexts. Our framework consists of two complementary modules: a switching policy learned via hierarchical RL with an expert guidance from sliding-horizon policy pre-evaluation, and an option-aware VQ-VAE that predicts option preference from discrete motion token sequences for improved generalization. The final decision is obtained via confidence-weighted fusion of two modules. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot demonstrate that BAT enables versatile long-horizon loco-manipulation and outperforms prior methods across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [141] [VRUD: A Drone Dataset for Complex Vehicle-VRU Interactions within Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01134)
*Ziyu Wang,Hongrui Kou,Cheng Wang,Ruochen Li,Hubert P. H. Shum,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei,Yuxin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无人机采集的高质量数据集VRUD，专注于城市复杂混合交通环境中车辆与弱势道路使用者的交互行为，填补现有数据集在非结构化城市环境中的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4级自动驾驶在城市复杂混合交通环境中面临巨大挑战，主要源于弱势道路使用者的高密度和不可预测的交互行为。现有开源数据集主要关注高速公路或规范交叉口等结构化场景，缺乏对混乱、非结构化城市环境的数据。

Method: 提出高效、高精度的无人机数据集构建方法，在深圳典型"城中村"采集数据，这些区域交通监管松散、遮挡严重。数据集包含4小时4K/30Hz视频，提取了11,479个VRU轨迹和1,939个车辆轨迹，并基于新颖的矢量碰撞时间阈值提取了4,002个多智能体交互场景。

Result: 建立了VRUD数据集，其特点是VRU占比约87%，远超现有基准。数据集提供标准OpenDRIVE高清地图支持，包含大量边缘案例，为增强自动驾驶系统在复杂非结构化城市环境中的安全性能提供宝贵资源。

Conclusion: VRUD数据集填补了现有数据在非结构化城市环境中的空白，为研究车辆与弱势道路使用者的复杂交互提供了独特资源，有助于提升自动驾驶系统在边缘案例中的安全性能。数据集已开源。

Abstract: The Operational Design Domain (ODD) of urbanoriented Level 4 (L4) autonomous driving, especially for autonomous robotaxis, confronts formidable challenges in complex urban mixed traffic environments. These challenges stem mainly from the high density of Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) and their highly uncertain and unpredictable interaction behaviors. However, existing open-source datasets predominantly focus on structured scenarios such as highways or regulated intersections, leaving a critical gap in data representing chaotic, unstructured urban environments. To address this, this paper proposes an efficient, high-precision method for constructing drone-based datasets and establishes the Vehicle-Vulnerable Road User Interaction Dataset (VRUD), as illustrated in Figure 1. Distinct from prior works, VRUD is collected from typical "Urban Villages" in Shenzhen, characterized by loose traffic supervision and extreme occlusion. The dataset comprises 4 hours of 4K/30Hz recording, containing 11,479 VRU trajectories and 1,939 vehicle trajectories. A key characteristic of VRUD is its composition: VRUs account for about 87% of all traffic participants, significantly exceeding the proportions in existing benchmarks. Furthermore, unlike datasets that only provide raw trajectories, we extracted 4,002 multi-agent interaction scenarios based on a novel Vector Time to Collision (VTTC) threshold, supported by standard OpenDRIVE HD maps. This study provides valuable, rare edge-case resources for enhancing the safety performance of ADS in complex, unstructured urban environments. To facilitate further research, we have made the VRUD dataset open-source at: https://zzi4.github.io/VRUD/.

</details>


### [142] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation under Distribution Shift with Bounded Extremum Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01142)
*Shaifalee Saxena,Rafael Fierro,Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合强化学习与有界极值搜索的混合控制器，提升机器人操作任务在分布外条件下的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作中表现良好，但训练分布与测试条件不同时性能会下降，特别是在接触丰富的任务中（如推动、抓取放置），目标、接触条件或机器人动力学的变化都会导致推理时超出分布范围

Method: 提出混合控制器，将深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）与有界极值搜索（bounded ES）结合。DDPG在标准条件下训练机器人推动和抓取放置任务，部署时与有界ES结合。RL策略提供快速操作行为，有界ES确保在操作条件偏离训练分布时控制器的鲁棒性

Result: 在多种分布外设置下评估控制器，包括时变目标和空间变化的摩擦斑块

Conclusion: 结合强化学习与有界极值搜索的混合方法能有效提升机器人操作任务在分布外条件下的鲁棒性

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in robotic manipulation, but learned policies often degrade in performance when test conditions differ from the training distribution. This limitation is especially important in contact-rich tasks such as pushing and pick-and-place, where changes in goals, contact conditions, or robot dynamics can drive the system out-of-distribution at inference time. In this paper, we investigate a hybrid controller that combines reinforcement learning with bounded extremum seeking to improve robustness under such conditions. In the proposed approach, deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) policies are trained under standard conditions on the robotic pushing and pick-and-place tasks, and are then combined with bounded ES during deployment. The RL policy provides fast manipulation behavior, while bounded ES ensures robustness of the overall controller to time variations when operating conditions depart from those seen during training. The resulting controller is evaluated under several out-of-distribution settings, including time-varying goals and spatially varying friction patches.

</details>


### [143] [SMASH: Mastering Scalable Whole-Body Skills for Humanoid Ping-Pong with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01158)
*Junli Ren,Yinghui Li,Kai Zhang,Penglin Fu,Haoran Jiang,Yixuan Pan,Guangjun Zeng,Tao Huang,Weizhong Guo,Peng Lu,Tianyu Li,Jingbo Wang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个名为\methodname的模块化系统，实现仅使用机载感知的人形机器人乒乓球连续击球，通过生成模型增强多样化击球动作，实现敏捷的全身协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人乒乓球系统依赖外部传感且无法实现敏捷的全身协调，主要挑战在于：1）快速运动下的低延迟机载感知；2）缺乏多样化的任务对齐击球动作来学习精确自然的全身行为。

Method: 提出模块化系统\methodname，结合可扩展的全身技能学习与机载感知。使用生成模型增强和多样化击球动作，建立可扩展的运动先验，实现紧密协调的全身控制而非解耦的上下半身行为。

Result: 实现了首个仅使用机载感知就能完成连续击球的人形机器人乒乓球系统。在高速条件下展示了稳定精确的球交换，包括爆炸性全身扣杀和低蹲击球等多样化动作。

Conclusion: 该系统验证了可扩展的感知驱动全身技能学习在动态人形交互任务中的有效性，为仅依赖机载感知实现敏捷人形机器人交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing humanoid table tennis systems remain limited by their reliance on external sensing and their inability to achieve agile whole-body coordination for precise task execution. These limitations stem from two core challenges: achieving low-latency and robust onboard egocentric perception under fast robot motion, and obtaining sufficiently diverse task-aligned strike motions for learning precise yet natural whole-body behaviors. In this work, we present \methodname, a modular system for agile humanoid table tennis that unifies scalable whole-body skill learning with onboard egocentric perception, eliminating the need for external cameras during deployment. Our work advances prior humanoid table-tennis systems in three key aspects. First, we achieve agile and precise ball interaction with tightly coordinated whole-body control, rather than relying on decoupled upper- and lower-body behaviors. This enables the system to exhibit diverse strike motions, including explosive whole-body smashes and low crouching shots. Second, by augmenting and diversifying strike motions with a generative model, our framework benefits from scalable motion priors and produces natural, robust striking behaviors across a wide workspace. Third, to the best of our knowledge, we demonstrate the first humanoid table-tennis system capable of consecutive strikes using onboard sensing alone, despite the challenges of low-latency perception, ego-motion-induced instability, and limited field of view. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate stable and precise ball exchanges under high-speed conditions, validating scalable, perception-driven whole-body skill learning for dynamic humanoid interaction tasks.

</details>


### [144] [A ROS 2 Wrapper for Florence-2: Multi-Mode Local Vision-Language Inference for Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01179)
*J. E. Domínguez-Vidal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个ROS 2包装器，将Florence-2视觉语言模型集成到机器人软件栈中，支持三种交互模式，可在消费级硬件上本地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视觉语言模型在机器人领域越来越重要，但实际应用需要可复现的中间件集成，而不仅仅是模型质量。Florence-2因其统一多种视觉语言任务且模型大小相对可控而具有吸引力。

Method: 开发了Florence-2的ROS 2包装器，提供三种交互模式：连续主题驱动处理、同步服务调用和异步动作。支持本地执行，包括原生安装和Docker容器部署，并整合通用JSON输出和标准ROS 2消息绑定。

Result: 进行了功能验证和多个GPU上的吞吐量研究，表明在消费级硬件上本地部署是可行的。代码仓库已公开。

Conclusion: 该ROS 2包装器使Florence-2模型能够实际集成到机器人软件栈中，为机器人感知提供了更丰富的语义能力，同时保持了部署的实用性。

Abstract: Foundation vision-language models are becoming increasingly relevant to robotics because they can provide richer semantic perception than narrow task-specific pipelines. However, their practical adoption in robot software stacks still depends on reproducible middleware integrations rather than on model quality alone. Florence-2 is especially attractive in this regard because it unifies captioning, optical character recognition, open-vocabulary detection, grounding and related vision-language tasks within a comparatively manageable model size. This article presents a ROS 2 wrapper for Florence-2 that exposes the model through three complementary interaction modes: continuous topic-driven processing, synchronous service calls and asynchronous actions. The wrapper is designed for local execution and supports both native installation and Docker container deployment. It also combines generic JSON outputs with standard ROS 2 message bindings for detection-oriented tasks. A functional validation is reported together with a throughput study on several GPUs, showing that local deployment is feasible with consumer grade hardware. The repository is publicly available here: https://github.com/JEDominguezVidal/florence2_ros2_wrapper

</details>


### [145] [Collaborative Task and Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robotic Teams using Multi-Agent PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01213)
*Matthias Rubio,Julia Richter,Hendrik Kolvenbach,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于MAPPO的多智能体协作规划策略，用于协调异构机器人团队解决复杂目标分配与调度问题，相比传统规划算法具有更好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的外星探索需要机器人团队协作，但传统规划算法在问题规模增大时计算成本呈组合增长，学习型方法将扩展性从运行时转移到训练时，为实现实时规划提供可能。

Method: 采用多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）方法，协调异构机器人团队解决复杂的目标分配和调度问题。

Result: 与穷举搜索获得的单目标最优解进行基准测试，并在行星探索场景中评估其在线重新规划能力。

Conclusion: MAPPO为基础的协作规划策略为解决机器人团队协调问题提供了可行方案，相比传统方法具有更好的可扩展性，为实现实时规划迈出关键一步。

Abstract: Efficient robotic extraterrestrial exploration requires robots with diverse capabilities, ranging from scientific measurement tools to advanced locomotion. A robotic team enables the distribution of tasks over multiple specialized subsystems, each providing specific expertise to complete the mission. The central challenge lies in efficiently coordinating the team to maximize utilization and the extraction of scientific value. Classical planning algorithms scale poorly with problem size, leading to long planning cycles and high inference costs due to the combinatorial growth of possible robot-target allocations and possible trajectories. Learning-based methods are a viable alternative that move the scaling concern from runtime to training time, setting a critical step towards achieving real-time planning. In this work, we present a collaborative planning strategy based on Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) to coordinate a team of heterogeneous robots to solve a complex target allocation and scheduling problem. We benchmark our approach against single-objective optimal solutions obtained through exhaustive search and evaluate its ability to perform online replanning in the context of a planetary exploration scenario.

</details>


### [146] [Functional Force-Aware Retargeting from Virtual Human Demos to Soft Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01224)
*Uksang Yoo,Mengjia Zhu,Evan Pezent,Jom Preechayasomboon,Jean Oh,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Amir Memar,Ben Abbatematteo,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Ashish Deshpande,Harsha Prahlad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftAct：一个通过显式推理接触力来教授软体机器人手执行类人操作技能的框架，利用VR捕捉人类演示并进行力感知重定向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅重定向人类关节轨迹，无法有效处理软体机器人手与人类手之间的极端形态不匹配和非线性柔顺性，需要显式建模接触几何和力分布

Method: 两阶段力感知重定向算法：第一阶段将演示的接触力分配到人类手指并按比例分配给机器人手指，建立力平衡映射；第二阶段结合末端执行器姿态跟踪和测地加权接触细化进行在线重定向

Result: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，SoftAct控制器将指尖轨迹跟踪RMSE降低达55%，跟踪方差降低达69%；在策略层面，在零样本真实世界部署和仿真中均获得更高成功率

Conclusion: 显式建模接触几何和力分布对于向软体机器人手有效传递技能至关重要，仅通过运动学模仿无法恢复这些信息，SoftAct框架能自然适应极端形态不匹配和非线性柔顺性

Abstract: We introduce SoftAct, a framework for teaching soft robot hands to perform human-like manipulation skills by explicitly reasoning about contact forces. Leveraging immersive virtual reality, our system captures rich human demonstrations, including hand kinematics, object motion, dense contact patches, and detailed contact force information. Unlike conventional approaches that retarget human joint trajectories, SoftAct employs a two-stage, force-aware retargeting algorithm. The first stage attributes demonstrated contact forces to individual human fingers and allocates robot fingers proportionally, establishing a force-balanced mapping between human and robot hands. The second stage performs online retargeting by combining baseline end-effector pose tracking with geodesic-weighted contact refinements, using contact geometry and force magnitude to adjust robot fingertip targets in real time. This formulation enables soft robotic hands to reproduce the functional intent of human demonstrations while naturally accommodating extreme embodiment mismatch and nonlinear compliance. We evaluate SoftAct on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks using a custom non-anthropomorphic pneumatic soft robot hand. SoftAct's controller reduces fingertip trajectory tracking RMSE by up to 55 percent and reduces tracking variance by up to 69 percent compared to kinematic and learning-based baselines. At the policy level, SoftAct achieves consistently higher success in zero-shot real-world deployment and in simulation. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling contact geometry and force distribution is essential for effective skill transfer to soft robotic hands, and cannot be recovered through kinematic imitation alone. Project videos and additional details are available at https://soft-act.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [How Emotion Shapes the Behavior of LLMs and Agents: A Mechanistic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00005)
*Moran Sun,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Zhenhong Zhou,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: E-STEER框架通过将情感作为结构化可控变量嵌入LLM隐藏状态，研究情感对推理、生成、安全和多步代理行为的影响，发现情感与行为存在符合心理学理论的非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感感知研究主要将情感视为表面风格因素或感知目标，忽视了其在任务处理中的机制作用。本文旨在探究情感信号是否能塑造大语言模型和代理的行为。

Method: 提出E-STEER框架，将情感作为结构化可控变量嵌入LLM隐藏状态，实现表示层面的直接干预，研究情感对客观推理、主观生成、安全性和多步代理行为的影响。

Result: 发现情感与行为存在非单调关系，符合心理学理论；特定情感不仅能增强LLM能力，还能提高安全性，并系统性地塑造多步代理行为。

Conclusion: E-STEER框架为理解情感在LLM和代理中的机制作用提供了可解释的方法，展示了情感干预在提升模型能力和安全性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Emotion plays an important role in human cognition and performance. Motivated by this, we investigate whether analogous emotional signals can shape the behavior of large language models (LLMs) and agents. Existing emotion-aware studies mainly treat emotion as a surface-level style factor or a perception target, overlooking its mechanistic role in task processing. To address this limitation, we propose E-STEER, an interpretable emotion steering framework that enables direct representation-level intervention in LLMs and agents. It embeds emotion as a structured, controllable variable in hidden states, and with it, we examine the impact of emotion on objective reasoning, subjective generation, safety, and multi-step agent behaviors. The results reveal non-monotonic emotion-behavior relations consistent with established psychological theories, and show that specific emotions not only enhance LLM capability but also improve safety, and systematically shape multi-step agent behaviors.

</details>


### [148] [One Panel Does Not Fit All: Case-Adaptive Multi-Agent Deliberation for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00085)
*Yuxing Lu,Yushuhong Lin,Jason Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAMP：自适应多智能体医疗诊断框架，通过主治医生动态组建专家小组，使用三值投票和混合路由机制，在复杂病例中提高诊断准确性并提供透明决策审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测方法存在局限性：单智能体策略只从一个角色分布采样，多智能体框架使用固定角色和平板多数投票，忽视了诊断分歧中的信号。复杂病例在轻微提示变化下会产生不同预测，需要更灵活、透明的决策机制。

Method: 提出CAMP框架：1) 主治医生智能体根据病例诊断不确定性动态组建专家小组；2) 每位专家使用三值投票（保留/拒绝/中立），允许在专业领域外弃权；3) 混合路由机制：强共识路径、主治医生判断回退路径、基于证据的仲裁路径（考虑论证质量而非票数）。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上的诊断预测和简短病程生成任务中，CAMP在四种LLM骨干网络上均优于强基线方法，同时比大多数竞争性多智能体方法消耗更少的token。投票记录和仲裁痕迹提供了透明的决策审计。

Conclusion: CAMP通过动态专家小组、三值投票和混合路由机制，有效处理临床预测中的病例异质性，在提高准确性的同时提供决策透明度，为复杂医疗诊断提供了更可靠的AI辅助框架。

Abstract: Large language models applied to clinical prediction exhibit case-level heterogeneity: simple cases yield consistent outputs, while complex cases produce divergent predictions under minor prompt changes. Existing single-agent strategies sample from one role-conditioned distribution, and multi-agent frameworks use fixed roles with flat majority voting, discarding the diagnostic signal in disagreement. We propose CAMP (Case-Adaptive Multi-agent Panel), where an attending-physician agent dynamically assembles a specialist panel tailored to each case's diagnostic uncertainty. Each specialist evaluates candidates via three-valued voting (KEEP/REFUSE/NEUTRAL), enabling principled abstention outside one's expertise. A hybrid router directs each diagnosis through strong consensus, fallback to the attending physician's judgment, or evidence-based arbitration that weighs argument quality over vote counts. On diagnostic prediction and brief hospital course generation from MIMIC-IV across four LLM backbones, CAMP consistently outperforms strong baselines while consuming fewer tokens than most competing multi-agent methods, with voting records and arbitration traces offering transparent decision audits.

</details>


### [149] [Open, Reliable, and Collective: A Community-Driven Framework for Tool-Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00137)
*Hy Dang,Quang Dao,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenTools是一个社区驱动的工具箱，通过标准化工具模式、提供轻量级包装器、自动化测试套件和持续监控来提升LLM工具使用的可靠性，强调工具内在准确性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具集成LLM的可靠性存在瓶颈，失败不仅源于工具使用准确性（代理调用工具的能力），还源于工具内在准确性（工具自身的正确性），而大多数先前工作只关注前者。

Method: 开发OpenTools工具箱，包含：1）标准化工具模式；2）轻量级即插即用包装器；3）自动化测试套件和持续监控工具评估；4）公共Web演示，用户可运行预定义代理和工具，贡献测试用例；5）核心框架、初始工具集、评估管道和贡献协议。

Result: 实验评估显示端到端可重复性和任务性能得到改善；社区贡献的、更高质量的任务特定工具在多个下游任务和基准测试中，相比现有工具箱带来6%-22%的相对性能提升，突显了工具内在准确性的重要性。

Conclusion: OpenTools通过社区驱动的方法解决了工具集成LLM的可靠性问题，强调工具内在准确性与工具使用准确性同等重要，为工具评估和贡献提供了标准化框架。

Abstract: Tool-integrated LLMs can retrieve, compute, and take real-world actions via external tools, but reliability remains a key bottleneck. We argue that failures stem from both tool-use accuracy (how well an agent invokes a tool) and intrinsic tool accuracy (the tool's own correctness), while most prior work emphasizes the former. We introduce OpenTools, a community-driven toolbox that standardizes tool schemas, provides lightweight plug-and-play wrappers, and evaluates tools with automated test suites and continuous monitoring. We also release a public web demo where users can run predefined agents and tools and contribute test cases, enabling reliability reports to evolve as tools change. OpenTools includes the core framework, an initial tool set, evaluation pipelines, and a contribution protocol. Experiments and evaluations show improved end-to-end reproducibility and task performance; community-contributed, higher-quality task-specific tools deliver 6%-22% relative gains over an existing toolbox across multiple agent architectures on downstream tasks and benchmarks, highlighting the importance of intrinsic tool accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [A Safety-Aware Role-Orchestrated Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Behavioral Health Communication Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00249)
*Ha Na Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个安全感知、角色编排的多智能体LLM框架，用于模拟支持性行为健康对话，通过协调的、角色分化的智能体来分解对话职责，包括共情导向、行动导向和监督角色，同时通过提示控制器动态激活相关智能体并执行持续安全审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体大型语言模型系统难以同时支持多样化的对话功能并在行为健康通信中保持安全性。需要一种能够协调不同对话角色并确保安全性的多智能体框架。

Method: 提出一个角色编排的多智能体LLM框架，将对话职责分解到专门的智能体（共情导向、行动导向、监督角色），使用基于提示的控制器动态激活相关智能体并执行持续安全审计。使用DAIC-WOZ语料库的半结构化访谈转录进行评估。

Result: 结果显示了清晰的角色分化、连贯的智能体间协调，以及与单智能体基线相比，在模块化编排、安全监督和响应延迟之间可预测的权衡。框架在结构质量、功能多样性和计算特性方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 该工作强调系统设计、可解释性和安全性，将该框架定位为行为健康信息学和决策支持研究的模拟分析工具，而非临床干预手段。

Abstract: Single-agent large language model (LLM) systems struggle to simultaneously support diverse conversational functions and maintain safety in behavioral health communication. We propose a safety-aware, role-orchestrated multi-agent LLM framework designed to simulate supportive behavioral health dialogue through coordinated, role-differentiated agents. Conversational responsibilities are decomposed across specialized agents, including empathy-focused, action-oriented, and supervisory roles, while a prompt-based controller dynamically activates relevant agents and enforces continuous safety auditing. Using semi-structured interview transcripts from the DAIC-WOZ corpus, we evaluate the framework with scalable proxy metrics capturing structural quality, functional diversity, and computational characteristics. Results illustrate clear role differentiation, coherent inter-agent coordination, and predictable trade-offs between modular orchestration, safety oversight, and response latency when compared to a single-agent baseline. This work emphasizes system design, interpretability, and safety, positioning the framework as a simulation and analysis tool for behavioral health informatics and decision-support research rather than a clinical intervention.

</details>


### [151] [Human-in-the-Loop Control of Objective Drift in LLM-Assisted Computer Science Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00281)
*Mark Dranias,Adam Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于系统工程和控制理论的人类在环(HITL)编程教育方法，通过将目标与世界模型作为可配置的操作工件，训练学生在AI辅助编程中保持控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助编程工具在计算机科学教育中存在"目标漂移"问题——局部合理的输出偏离任务规范。现有教学方法过于依赖特定工具的提示技巧，缺乏对AI工具演进的适应性。需要建立稳定的人类在环控制教育框架。

Method: 采用系统工程和控制理论视角，将目标和世界模型作为可配置的操作工件。设计本科CS实验课程，明确分离规划与执行阶段，训练学生先制定验收标准和架构约束再进行代码生成。部分实验还引入概念对齐的故意漂移来支持诊断和恢复。

Result: 提出了三组对比实验设计（非结构化AI使用、结构化规划、结构化规划+注入漂移）的敏感性功效分析，确定了在现实班级规模约束下可检测的效应量。建立了理论驱动、方法明确的人类在环教学基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为人类在环教学提供了理论驱动的方法论基础，使控制能力成为可教授的技能，能够适应不断演进的AI工具，而不是将人类参与视为向AI自主的过渡步骤。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in computer science education through AI-assisted programming tools, yet such workflows often exhibit objective drift, in which locally plausible outputs diverge from stated task specifications. Existing instructional responses frequently emphasize tool-specific prompting practices, limiting durability as AI platforms evolve. This paper adopts a human-centered stance, treating human-in-the-loop (HITL) control as a stable educational problem rather than a transitional step toward AI autonomy. Drawing on systems engineering and control-theoretic concepts, we frame objectives and world models as operational artifacts that students configure to stabilize AI-assisted work. We propose a pilot undergraduate CS laboratory curriculum that explicitly separates planning from execution and trains students to specify acceptance criteria and architectural constraints prior to code generation. In selected labs, the curriculum also introduces deliberate, concept-aligned drift to support diagnosis and recovery from specification violations. We report a sensitivity power analysis for a three-arm pilot design comparing unstructured AI use, structured planning, and structured planning with injected drift, establishing detectable effect sizes under realistic section-level constraints. The contribution is a theory-driven, methodologically explicit foundation for HITL pedagogy that renders control competencies teachable across evolving AI tools.

</details>


### [152] [Improvisational Games as a Benchmark for Social Intelligence of AI Agents: The Case of Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00284)
*Gaurav Rajesh Parikh,Angikar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Connections游戏作为评估AI智能体社交推理能力的基准，要求智能体具备知识检索、总结和认知状态感知能力，超越单纯记忆和演绎推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体在需要社交智能的任务中的推理能力，特别是超越自身记忆和演绎推理，涉及理解其他智能体认知状态的能力。

Method: 引入Connections即兴文字游戏作为评估框架，要求智能体在受限环境中与其他智能体沟通，展示知识检索、总结和认知状态感知能力。

Result: Connections游戏被证明是评估语言模型智能体社交智能能力的良好基准，能够测试智能体在协作游戏中展示社交意识和智能的能力。

Conclusion: Connections游戏为评估AI智能体社交推理能力提供了有效框架，强调了在协作环境中沟通和认知状态理解的重要性。

Abstract: We formally introduce a improvisational wordplay game called Connections to explore reasoning capabilities of AI agents. Playing Connections combines skills in knowledge retrieval, summarization and awareness of cognitive states of other agents. We show how the game serves as a good benchmark for social intelligence abilities of language model based agents that go beyond the agents' own memory and deductive reasoning and also involve gauging the understanding capabilities of other agents. Finally, we show how through communication with other agents in a constrained environment, AI agents must demonstrate social awareness and intelligence in games involving collaboration.

</details>


### [153] [Collaborative AI Agents and Critics for Fault Detection and Cause Analysis in Network Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00319)
